Coverage and limitations

  • vllm-h100 / First useful output: Timing observed; no acceptance limit configured. To conclude, set limits.first_output_ms to your service's acceptance target and rerun.
  • vllm-h100 / Generation pace: Timing observed; no acceptance limit configured. To conclude, set limits.latency_ms or limits.stream_gap_ms or limits.min_output_tokens_per_second to your service's acceptance target and rerun.
  • vllm-h100 / Arrival traffic capacity: The full rate plan needs more delivered, scored or timely evidence. To conclude, allow traffic.duration_seconds of at least 5 seconds per rate for at least 5 scored completions; set limits.first_output_ms or limits.latency_ms, enable stream/token measurements, and allow the whole rate plan to finish. Raise max_requests (or --max-requests) above 320 if exhausted, or select fewer checks. The plan needs at least 138 fixed requests; adaptive traffic may need more.
  • vllm-h100 / Concurrent workloads: Scenario results: Input/output size and concurrency matrix: pass; Mixed workload interference: inconclusive; Streaming fairness: inconclusive; Token stress cases: inconclusive. Mixed workload interference: Mixed workload requires concurrency of at least two. To conclude, inspect the failed request and coverage details, correct the reported server or workload issue, then rerun the same workload.
  • vllm-h100 / Structured JSON: Not selected for this run.
  • vllm-h100 / Sustained load and recovery: Recovery observed; no acceptance limit configured. To conclude, set limits.recovery_max_ratio or limits.first_output_ms or limits.latency_ms or limits.stream_gap_ms or limits.min_output_tokens_per_second to your service's acceptance target and rerun.
  • vllm-h100 / GPU observations: GPU end scrape did not return fresh device samples; the observation window is incomplete. To conclude, collect at least 6 complete scored answers per tested condition; check the fixture's expected answer and evaluator errors, increase the output budget for truncated answers, then rerun.
  • Other traffic on the target is unknown; results cover only the recorded workload.
  • Backend/replica identity is unverified unless explicitly reported by the server; a service URL does not establish complete replica coverage.
  • Some checks have too few successful samples for confident timing conclusions.
